  super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);\n\n        serverId = getArguments().getInt(ARG_SERVER_ID);\n\n        // All settings of the app are stored in the shared preferences file used by Util,\n        // so the preference screen must read and write to the very same file. Otherwise\n        // the entered server settings would be stored in the default shared preferences\n        // file and would never be seen by the rest of the app.\n }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onPreferenceChange(Preference preference, Object newValue) {\n        if (preference == serverNamePref) {\n            sharedPreferences.edit()\n                    .putString(Constants.PREFERENCES_KEY_SERVER_NAME + serverId, (String) newValue)\n                    .apply();\n            updateName();\n            return true;\n        } else if (preference ==        throw new Exception();\n                }\n            } catch (Exception x) {\n                new ErrorDialog(getActivity(), R.string.settings_invalid_url, false);\n                return updateUrl();\n            return true;\n        } else if (preference == serverUsernamePref) {\n            String username = (String) newValue;\n            if (username == null || .putBoolean(Constants.PREFERENCES_KEY_SERVER_ENABLED + serverId, (Boolean) newValue)\n                    .apply();\n            return true;\n        } else if (preference == jukeboxPref) {\n .putBoolean(Constants.PREFERENCES_KEY_LDAP_SUPPORT + serverId, (Boolean) newValue)\n                    .apply();\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onPreferenceClick(Preference preference) {\n        if (preference == removeServerPref) {\n            removeServer();\n            return true;\n        } else if (preference == testConnectionPref) {\n            testConnection();\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private void setupPreferencesValues() {\n        updateName();\n        updateUrl();\n        updateUsername();\n        updatePassword();\n\n        if (!sharedPreferences.contains(Constants.PREFERENCES_KEY_SERVER_ENABLED + serverId)) {\n            sharedPreferences.edit()\n                    .putBoolean(Constants.PREFERENCES_KEY_SERVER_ENABLED + serverId, true)\n                    .apply();\n        }\n * <li><code>format(918)</code> returns <em>"918 B"</em>.</li>\n\t * <li><code>format(98765)</code> returns <em>"96 KB"</em>.</li>\n\t * <li><code>format(1238476)</code> returns <em>"1.2 MB"</em>.</li>\n\t * </ul>\n\t * This method assumes that 1 KB is 1024 bytes.\n\t * To get a localized string, please use formatLocalizedBytes instead.\n\t *\n\t * @param byteCount The number of bytes.\n\t * @return The formatted string.\n\t */\n\tpublic static synchronized String formatBytes(long byteCount)\n\t{\n\n\t\t// More than 1 GB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024 * 1024 * 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tNumberFormat gigaByteFormat = GIGA_BYTE_FORMAT;\n\t\t\treturn gigaByteFormat.format((double) byteCount / (1024 * 1024 * 1024));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// More than 1 MB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024 * 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tNumberFormat megaByteFormat = MEGA_BYTE_FORMAT;\n\t\t\treturn megaByteFormat.format((double) byteCount / (1024 * 1024));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// More than 1 KB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tNumberFormat kiloByteFormat = KILO_BYTE_FORMAT;\n\t\t\treturn kiloByteFormat.format((double) byteCount / 1024);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn byteCount + " B";\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Converts a byte-count to a formatted string suitable for display to the user.\n\t * For instance:\n\t * <ul>\n\t * <li><code>format(918)</code> returns <em>"918 B"</em>.</li>\n\t * <li><code>format(98765)</code> returns <em>"96 KB"</em>.</li>\n\t * <li><code>format(1238476)</code> returns <em>"1.2 MB"</em>.</li>\n\t * </ul>\n\t * This method assumes that 1 KB is 1024 bytes.\n\t * This version of the method returns a localized string.\n\t *\n\t * @param byteCount The number of bytes.\n\t * @return The formatted string.\n\t */\n\tpublic static synchronized String formatLocalizedBytes(long byteCount, Context context)\n\t{\n\n\t\t// More than 1 GB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024 * 1024 * 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tif (GIGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT == null)\n\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\tGIGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT = new DecimalFormat(context.getResources().getString(R.string.util_bytes_format_gigabyte));\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn GIGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT.format((double) byteCount / (1024 * 1024 * 1024));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// More than 1 MB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024 * 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tif (MEGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT == null)\n\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\tMEGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT = new DecimalFormat(context.getResources().getString(R.string.util_bytes_format_megabyte));\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn MEGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT.format((double) byteCount / (1024 * 1024));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// More than 1 KB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tif (KILO_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT == null)\n\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\tKILO_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT = new DecimalFormat(context.getResources().getString(R.string.util_bytes_format_kilobyte));\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn KILO_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT.format((double) byteCount / 1024);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT == null)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tBYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT = new DecimalFormat(context.getResources().getString(R.string.util_bytes_format_byte));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT.format((double) byteCount);\n\t}\n\n\tpublic static boolean equals(Object object1, Object object2)\n\t{\n\t\treturn object1 == object2 || !(object1 == null || object2 == null) && object1.equals(object2);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Encodes the given string by using the hexadecimal representation of its UTF-8 bytes.\n\t *\n\t * @param s The string to encode.\n\t * @return The encoded string.\n\t */\n\tpublic static String utf8HexEncode(String s)\n\t{\n\t\tif (s == null)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tbyte[] utf8;\n\n\t\ttry\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tutf8 = s.getBytes(Constants.UTF_8);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (UnsupportedEncodingException x)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(x);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn hexEncode(utf8);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Converts an array of bytes into an array of characters representing the hexadecimal values of each byte in order.\n\t * The returned array will be double the length of the passed array, as it takes two characters to represent any\n\t * given byte.\n\t *\n\t * @param data Bytes to convert to hexadecimal characters.\n\t * @return A string containing hexadecimal characters.\n\t */\n\tpublic static String hexEncode(byte[] data)\n\t{\n\t\tint length = data.length;\n\t\tchar[] out = new char[length << 1];\n\t\tint j = 0;\n\n\t\t// two characters form the hex value.\n\t\tfor (byte aData : data)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tout[j++] = HEX_DIGITS[(0xF0 & aData) >>> 4];\n\t\t\tout[j++] = HEX_DIGITS[0x0F & aData];\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn new String(out);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Calculates the MD5 digest and returns the value as a 32 character hex string.\n\t *\n\t * @param s Data to digest.\n\t * @return MD5 digest as a hex string.\n\t */\n\tpublic static String md5Hex(String s)\n\t{\n\t\tif (s == null)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttry\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tMessageDigest md5 = MessageDigest.getInstance("MD5");\n\t\t\treturn hexEncode(md5.digest(s.getBytes(Constants.UTF_8)));\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (Exception x)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tthrow new RuntimeException(x.getMessage(), x);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tpublic static String AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='The bug is fixed and the project builds successfully.\n\n## Root cause\n\nThe app stores/reads all its settings through `Util.getPreferences()`, which uses an explicitly named preferences file:\n\n```java\ncontext.getSharedPreferences(Constants.PREFERENCES_FILE_NAME, 0);  // "org.moire.ultrasonic_preferences"\n```\n\nBut the new preference screens (`ServerSettingsFragment`, `SettingsFragment`) used `PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences()`, which resolves to `<applicationId>_preferences`. For the debug build (`applicationIdSuffix ".debug"`) that is `org.moire.ultrasonic.debug_preferences` — a **different file**.\n\nSo the server name/URL/user/password entered in the settings screen were written to a file nobody else reads. When `MusicServiceFactory.createSubsonicApiClient()` looked them up via `Util.getPreferences()`, it got `null` and fell back to:\n\n```java\nLog.i("MusicServiceFactory", "Server credentials is not available");\nreturn new SubsonicAPIClient("http://localhost", "", "", ...);\n```\n\nwhich is exactly the `Failed to connect to localhost/127.0.0.1:80` in the log. The same mismatch broke the main settings screen (theme, cache, bitrate, …) and the server list.\n\n## Changes\n\n1. **`fragment/ServerSettingsFragment.java`**\n   - `getPreferenceManager().setSharedPreferencesName(Constants.PREFERENCES_FILE_NAME)` before inflating the preference screen, and `sharedPreferences = Util.getPreferences(getActivity())` instead of the default prefs — server settings now land in the file the app actually reads.\n   - Added `invalidateMusicService()` (`MusicServiceFactory.resetMusicService()`) when URL/username/password/self-signed/LDAP change or a server is removed, so the cached `MusicService` (and its `SubsonicAPIClient`) is rebuilt with the new credentials instead of a stale one.\n\n2. **`fragment/SettingsFragment.java`** — same preferences-file fix (`setSharedPreferencesName` + `Util.getPreferences`), so the general settings are persisted where the app reads them.\n\n3. **`activity/MainActivity.java`** — `PreferenceManager.setDefaultValues(this, Constants.PREFERENCES_FILE_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE, R.xml.settings, false)` so XML defaults are seeded into the app\'s preferences file rather than the default one.\n\n4. **`util/Util.java`** — `getActiveServers()` now reads from `getPreferences(context)` (the app file, default `0` matching what the settings screen writes) instead of the default shared preferences; dropped the now-unused `PreferenceManager` import.\n\nVerified with `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` (JDK 8, since Gradle 4.8.1 can\'t run on the default JDK 17): **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 349, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 78505, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 349, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 3, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfHugmVGCeN1gpkMaTX4h', stop_reason=None, session_id='dc7a7709-b825-4127-8edc-63b75d2451e7', uuid='61cbd1c2-cf22-41c6-8b81-801a9a19566e')
